<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">import</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> { useLocale } </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">from</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF"> "@/registry/base-nova/ui/locale-provider"</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">/*</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> * Compact text navigation for docs and settings sidebars: a search field, collapsible sections</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> * and groups, and rows that all start on one left edge. The current row is a fill plus a 2px bar</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> * in the gutter.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> *</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> * Icons: when a list (or a section's rows) holds a row or group label with an `icon`, the rows</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> * and labels beside it that have none move in by the icon's width, so every text starts on one</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> * edge. That happens on its own; set `--sidebar-nav-inset` on a wrapper to force or cancel it.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> *</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> *   &#x3C;SidebarNav></span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> *     &#x3C;SidebarNavSearch shortcut="⌘K" onClick={openSearch} /></span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> *     &#x3C;SidebarNavContent aria-label="Docs"></span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> *       &#x3C;SidebarNavSection label="Guides" count={3}></span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> *         &#x3C;SidebarNavList></span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> *           &#x3C;SidebarNavLink href="/docs" active>Introduction&#x3C;/SidebarNavLink></span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> *         &#x3C;/SidebarNavList></span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> *         &#x3C;SidebarNavSection level="group" label="Forms" href="/docs/forms">…&#x3C;/SidebarNavSection></span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> *       &#x3C;/SidebarNavSection></span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> *     &#x3C;/SidebarNavContent></span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> *     &#x3C;SidebarNavFooter>…&#x3C;/SidebarNavFooter></span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> *   &#x3C;/SidebarNav></span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> */</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">/*</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> * The icon-aligned inset. A container whose direct rows or group labels carry an icon sets</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> * `--sidebar-nav-inset` to the icon slot plus its gap (16px + 8px); rows and labels without an</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> * icon add it to their start padding. Nested groups inherit it, so their rows line up too.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> */</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">  }, [])</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">  return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> (</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">    &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#116329;--shiki-dark:#7EE787">nav</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">      ref</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">={</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">ref</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">}</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">      data-slot</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">"sidebar-nav-content"</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">      className</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">={</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF">cn</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">"scrollbar-hover min-h-0 flex-1 overflow-y-auto overscroll-contain px-3 pb-4"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">, className)</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">}</span></span>

<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">type</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657"> SidebarNavSectionProps</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657"> Omit</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">&#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">CollapsiblePrimitive</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">Root</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">Props</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">"children"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">> </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">&#x26;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">  label</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657"> React</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">ReactNode</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">  /** Muted count after the label. */</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">  count</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">?:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> number</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">  /** `section`: a top-level heading; `group`: a smaller muted heading inside a section. */</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">  level</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">?:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF"> "section"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> |</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF"> "group"</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">  /** A section with no toggle (always open). */</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">  collapsible</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">?:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> boolean</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">  /** Makes the label a link to the section's own page; the chevron still folds it. */</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">  href</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">?:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> string</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">  /** Renders the label link with your router's link, such as `&#x3C;Link href="…" />`. Implies a link label. */</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">  labelRender</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">?:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657"> useRender</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">ComponentProps</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">&#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">"a"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">>[</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">"render"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">]</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">  /** The label's page is current: the label row gets the active fill and `aria-current="page"`. */</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">  active</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">?:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> boolean</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">  /** Leading 16px icon, muted until the label is hovered or current (same slot as `SidebarNavLink`). */</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">  /** Name of the fold toggle on a link label. Defaults to the locale's "Toggle {label}" ("Toggle section" for a non-text label). */</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">  toggleLabel</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">?:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> string</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">function</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF"> SidebarNavList</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">({ </span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">className</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">...</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">props</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> }</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657"> React</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">ComponentProps</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">&#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">"ul"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">>) {</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">function</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF"> SidebarNavLink</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">({ </span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">active</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> false</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">badge</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">icon</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">className</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">children</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">render</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">...</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">props</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> }</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657"> SidebarNavLinkProps</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">) {</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">    render,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">    props: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF">mergeProps</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">&#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">"a"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">>(</span></span>
